# Evidence for task 16f4ea12-757c-418e-b5c5-a8320d39f8a7

> creacion deperfiles de agentes, un perfil tiene una lista de modelos que funcionan como principal + lista de fallbacks si un modelo no response ya rate limiting o algun otro error, debe pasar al primer fallback y asi hasta que se gaste todos los fallbacks y termina con error si ningun modelo responde. si en los settings esta desactivado un provedor, el modelo dentro del profile se desactiva. ahora cada proyecto tiene que seleccionar un profile en cada eatep en vez de un provider + model. debe haber uan nueva pestaña para configurar los agent profiles

One section per Run, written by LunasQ when it closes. It is not edited by hand: it is rewritten.

      "rationale": "Those are the cases where the model 'does not respond'; the rest are not attributable to the model and switching would not fix them",

      ]

    {
      "decision": "A start failure falls back inside the same Run (existing startAgentWithFallback mechanism, per model entry instead of per provider); a failure after launch closes the Run as failed with reason `model_fallback` (without consuming a retry) and dispatches a new Run of the same phase in the same worktree with the next model",
      "rationale": "Reuses the existing in-Run fallback and the resume-style relaunch; the work in the worktree is preserved",
      "discarded": [
        "Restarting the agent inside the same Run with another model"
      ]

      "decision": "Existing project configurations are migrated by creating one profile per distinct effective (kind, model, effort, args) and pointing each phase to it; a `Default` profile (Claude, no model) is seeded and used for new projects",

      "discarded": [
        "Resetting all projects to a default profile"

# ADR 0016: agent provider adapters

## Status

Accepted

## Context

The dispatcher must run Claude Code and GitHub Copilot CLI without mixing one provider's flags, MCP or telemetry with another's. Projects can select a provider per phase. Claude Code emits OTLP usage and HTTP subagent hooks; Copilot CLI does not offer those same signals in the current integration.

## Decision

- The explicit providers validated by the schema are Claude Code and Copilot CLI; a registry in `adapters` declares their capabilities and composes provider‑specific arguments.
- The primary configuration is per phase. The fallback tries each registered provider once, in order starting with the primary, only when the process cannot start or become ready before rece
… 21388 characters omitted …
ed`, `usage-limit`, `limit resets <time>`, etc.) and the screen is only read on a phase stop; if the agent's own work mentions limits with those exact forms, the escalation is recoverable in one click (resume) and the notification shows the text that triggered it.

## Consequences

- When Claude ends the turn because of the limit, LunasQ does not burn nudges or retries: the task stays in `needs_human` with the analysis state on screen.
- Detection depends on the TUI: if Claude changes its texts, `USAGE_LIMIT_PATTERN` in adapters must be updated; the `herdr agent read` spikes are the way to capture them.
- Test against real herdr/Claude pending for the pilot (complements the clock and nudge pending items in [uw-17](uw-17-reconciliacion.md)).
```

  /** Evidence is not part of the close: if it fails it is reported and the Run is closed anyway. */
  private async recordEvidence(runId: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.evidence?.recordRun(runId);
    } catch (error) {
      this.onError(asError(error), runId);
    }
  }

  private background(runId: string): void {
    this.launching.add(runId);
    const job = this.launch(runId).catch((error: unknown) => this.onError(asError(error), runId));
    this.inFlight.add(job);
    void job.finally(() => {
      this.inFlight.delete(job);
      this.launching.delete(runId);
    });
  }

  /** Throws RunClosed if the Run was closed while launching. */

  /** Phase timeout expired: needs_human with notification; the agent stays alive. */
  private async expire(w: Watch): Promise<void> {
    if (this.inPhase(w, "waiting_human")) {
      // It expired with the question open before the outbox paused the clock: it stays paused
      // without budget and expires as soon as the answer resumes it.
      w.clock = new PhaseClock(0, () => this.serial(w.runId, (w) => this.expire(w)));
      w.clock.pause("waiting_human");
      w.clock.start();
      return;
    }

    "docs/design.md",
    "docs/decisions/0016-proveedores-agente.md",
    "docs/decisions/settings-proveedores.md",
    "docs/decisions/effort-por-fase.md",
    "docs/decisions/0008-config-proyecto.md"
  ],
  "decisions": [
    {
      "decision": "Agent profiles are a global aggregate (own table `agent_profiles`), referenced by id from each project's phase configuration",
      "rationale": "The goal asks for a separate tab to configure them and for projects to select one per phase: a profile is shared across projects",
      "discarded": [
        "Profiles embedded in each project",
        "Keeping provider + model in the project and adding a fallback list per phase"
      ]
    },
    {
      "decision": "The fallback chain is only the profile's model list; the implicit fallback to the other providers in registry order (ADR 0016, settings-proveedores) is removed",
      "rationale": "The human declares the fallbacks explicitly; mixing both orders would be ambiguous",
      "discarded": [
        "Keeping the registry fallback after the profile's list is exhausted"
      ]
    },
    {
      "decision": "Fallback triggers: start failure, usage limit detected on screen, agent process exited / pane closed, and agent stopped without end-of-phase signal after the nudges. Other failures (hook blocked, profile_conflict, worktree errors, phase timeout) keep their current route",
      "rationale": "Those are the cases where the model 'does not respond'; switching would not fix the rest",
      "discarded": [
        "Falling back on any Run failure",
        "Falling back on phase timeout"
      ]
    },
    {
      "decision": "A start failure falls back inside the same Run (startAgentWithFallback, per model entry); a failure after launch closes the Run as failed with reason `model_fallback` (no retry consumed) and dispatches a new Run of the same phase in the same worktree with the next model",
      "rationale": "Reuses the existing in-Run fallback and the resume-style relaunch; the worktree work is preserved",
      "discarded": [
        "Restarting the agent inside the same Run with another model"
      ]
    },
    {
      "decision": "The migration discards the former per-phase provider configuration and leaves every phase of every existing project without a profile (null); no equivalent profiles and no `Default` profile are seeded",
      "rationale": "Human review of spec v1: «No fallback, current projects must be left without profiles after the change»",
      "discarded": [
        "Creating one equivalent profile per distinct former configuration (spec v1)",
        "Seeding a Default profile and pointing every phase to it"
      ]
    },
    {
      "decision": "A phase without profile is a dispatch block, not an error: the scheduler skips the task (it stays queued, no Run, no retry, no escalation), the claim rejects with `profile_missing`, and the UI warns until the human assigns a profile",
      "rationale": "The human asked for the problem to be caught gracefully and for the user to be warned and able to fix each project; it mirrors the existing all-providers-disabled pause (DispatchPausedBanner)",
      "discarded": [
        "Escalating the affected tasks to needs_human",
        "Failing the Run when it is about to start"
      ]
    },
    {
      "decision": "Saving a project emits a global `project.updated` event that wakes the scheduler",
      "rationale": "Assigning the missing profile must unblock the held tasks without waiting for an unrelated wake-up, like settings.updated does today",
      "discarded": [
        "Relying on the scheduler's next periodic pass"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "knownRisks": [
    "Usage-limit detection reads Claude Code's screen only (ADR 0015); for Copilot and Codex a rate limit is only detected when the agent exits or stops without signal",
    "Breaking change of the projects API: `agentProfiles` now carries nullable profile ids instead of provider configs; the web client and its fake API must change together",
    "Right after deploying, every existing project's tasks stop being dispatched until the human creates profiles and assigns them: this is intended but visible",
    "Runs live during the migration keep their recorded provider; if they later need a relaunch (model fallback, resume) and their phase has no profile, they wait in queued",
    "The fallback relaunch competes for concurrency slots like a new claim: if the next model's provider has no slot, the task waits in queued instead of failing"
  ],
  "phase": "refinement",
  "body": {
    "title": "Agent profiles with model fallbacks selected per project phase",
    "deliverable": "code",
    "domainModel": {
      "boundedContext": "Dispatch configuration (Projects, Settings and Dispatcher)",
      "glossary": [
        {
          "term": "Agent profile",
          "definition": "Named, global configuration with an ordered list of models; the first is the primary and the rest are fallbacks"
        },
        {
          "term": "Profile model",
          "definition": "One entry of an agent profile: provider (AgentKind), optional model, optional effort and extra args. Replaces the former per-phase provider + model configuration"
        },
        {
          "term": "Primary model",
          "definition": "The first profile model of the list"
        },
        {
          "term": "Fallback model",
          "definition": "Any profile model after the primary, tried in list order when the previous one fails"
        },
        {
          "term": "Disabled model",
          "definition": "A profile model whose provider is turned off in Settings; it is skipped and shown as disabled"
        },
        {
          "term": "Effective model",
          "definition": "The first profile model that is enabled and not yet exhausted in the current phase dispatch"
        },
        {
          "term": "Exhausted model",
          "definition": "A profile model that already failed (start failure, usage limit, exit or stop without signal) in the current phase dispatch"
        },
        {
          "term": "Phase dispatch",
          "definition": "The sequence of Runs of one phase from its entry (or a human resume) until it ends; models are exhausted within it and reset after it"
        },
        {
          "term": "Phase profile",
          "definition": "The agent profile a project selects for one phase (refine, implement, verify, deliver); it may be unassigned"
        },
        {
          "term": "Unassigned phase",
          "definition": "A project phase with no agent profile; tasks reaching it are held in queued until the human assigns one"
        },
        {
          "term": "Held task",
          "definition": "A queued task the scheduler does not dispatch because its next phase is unassigned"
        }
      ],
      "aggregates": [
        {
          "name": "AgentProfile",
          "entities": [
            "ProfileModel"
          ]
        },
        {
          "name": "Project",
          "entities": [
            "PhaseProfile reference (nullable)"
          ]
        },
        {
          "name": "Run",
          "entities": [
            "Profile model used (profile id + model index)"
          ]
        }
      ],
      "invariants": [
        "An agent profile has a unique, non-empty name and between 1 and 10 profile models",
        "A profile model with `effort` and `--effort` in args is rejected (effort-por-fase); Copilot and Codex reserved flags stay rejected (ADR 0016, codex-provider)",
        "A project phase references either an existing agent profile or none",
        "An agent profile referenced by any project cannot be deleted",
        "A task whose next phase is unassigned is never dispatched: no Run is created, no retry is consumed and it is not escalated",
        "Within one phase dispatch each profile model is tried at most once",
        "A disabled model is never started",
        "When all models of the phase profile are disabled, the phase is not dispatched and the task stays queued (no Run consumed)",
        "When all enabled models are exhausted, the task escalates to needs_human with the diagnostics of every attempt",
        "Changing a profile, a project or Settings never interrupts a live Run; it applies to the next dispatch or launch",
        "A fallback after launch does not consume a retry of run_failed"
      ],
      "events": [
        {
          "name": "agent_profile.created",
          "description": "A profile was created (global event, taskId null); wakes the scheduler"
        },
        {
          "name": "agent_profile.updated",
          "description": "A profile's name or models changed (global event); wakes the scheduler to replan with the new effective model"
        },
        {
          "name": "agent_profile.deleted",
          "description": "An unused profile was deleted (global event)"
        },
        {
          "name": "project.updated",
          "description": "New global event: a project's configuration (including its phase profiles) was saved; wakes the scheduler so held tasks are reconsidered"
        },
        {
          "name": "run.provider_attempt",
          "description": "Existing event, now also carrying the model index and model of the profile entry attempted"
        },
        {
          "name": "run.model_fallback",
          "description": "A launched Run failed because of its model (usage limit, exit, stop without signal) and the phase moves to the next model; carries runId, phase, model index and reason"
        },
        {
          "name": "task.escalated (providers_unavailable)",
          "description": "Existing escalation, emitted when the profile's enabled models are all exhausted"
        }
      ]
    },
    "requirements": [
      "The human can create, list, edit and delete agent profiles from a new «Perfiles» / «Profiles» tab in the navigation",
      "A profile has a name and an ordered list of models (provider, optional model, optional effort, optional args); the human can add, remove and reorder them",
      "Each project selects one agent profile per phase instead of provider, model, effort and args; a phase may be left unassigned",
      "The migration removes the former per-phase provider configuration: every phase of every existing project ends up unassigned, and no profile is created",
      "A task whose next phase is unassigned stays queued without a Run; the claim rejects with profile_missing",
      "The UI warns about unassigned phases: a global banner while any project has an unassigned phase (linking to Projects), a badge per affected project in the Projects page, a warning per unassigned phase in the project form, and a notice in the detail of a held task naming the project and phase",
      "Assigning a profile to an unassigned phase emits project.updated and the held tasks are dispatched on the next scheduler pass",
      "The dispatcher starts the phase with the effective model of the phase profile",
      "If a model fails to start, runs out of usage, exits or stops without the end-of-phase signal after the nudges, the phase continues with the next enabled model of the profile",
      "When no enabled model remains, the Run fails and the task goes to needs_human with an error listing each model's failure",
      "Models whose provider is disabled in Settings are skipped by the scheduler, the claim and the launch, and appear as disabled in the Profiles tab and in the project form",
      "A profile in use by a project cannot be deleted; the UI and the API say which projects use it",
      "The verification phase keeps the Sonnet default when its effective model is Claude without a model",
      "API: GET/POST /api/agent-profiles and GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/agent-profiles/:id, with Zod validation in schemas and the local-origin guard used by /api/settings",
      "All new UI texts exist in the ES and EN dictionaries",
      "A new ADR docs/decisions/agent-profiles.md records the decision, and ADR 0008, ADR 0016, settings-proveedores, effort-por-fase and docs/design.md are updated"

    "criteria": [

### Verdict

- Verdict: **reject**
- Risk: medium

#### Criteria

- AC-1 · pass · Profile create/list tests pass; reversal of the name-conflict code fails the db tests (conflict code changed).
- AC-2 · pass · Reversal of the conflict code fails 2 db repository tests (repeated name, 0 or >10 models).
- AC-3 · pass · Reversal of the effort/--effort guard fails 2 adapters tests naming the flag.
- AC-4 · pass · Reversal of the in-use guard fails the db test and the server API 409 test.
- AC-5 · pass · Reversal of the delete statement fails the db test that records agent_profile.deleted.
- AC-6 · pass · Reversal of the profile_not_found mapping fails the POST and PATCH server tests.
- AC-7 · pass · Reversal removing the no-profile option fails 3 web project tests.
- AC-8 · pass · Reversal of the 0018 migration UPDATE fails schema tests; the migration test names the clearing of every phase.
- AC-9 · pass · Reversal of the unassigned resolve fails 3 domain tests (unassigned phase).
- AC-10 · pass · Reversal of the claim's profile_missing check fails the db runs test.
- AC-11 · pass · Reversal of the banner's empty check fails 3 banner tests.
- AC-12 · pass · Reversal hiding the missing-profile badge fails the projects test.
- AC-13 · pass · Reversal hiding the hold notice fails 2 task-detail tests.
- AC-14 · pass · Reversal of the project.updated event type fails the db projects test.
- AC-15 · pass · Partial reversal: the skip of exhausted and disabled models fails scheduler and agent-profile tests; the primary-start Run test was not reversed separately.
- AC-16 · pass · Reversal forcing model index 1 out of the start chain fails 6 dispatcher tests.
- AC-17 · pass · Reversal reporting model failure as unavailable fails 4 watcher and dispatcher tests.
- AC-18 · pass · Same reversal as AC-17 fails the watcher tests for exit and stop without signal.
- AC-19 · pass · Reversal removing the exhausted-models skip fails the domain and scheduler tests; the dispatcher escalation test also fails under the same reversal.
- AC-20 · pass · Reversal removing the disabled-model skip fails 6 domain and scheduler tests.
- AC-21 · pass · Same reversal as AC-20 fails the all-disabled hold test.
- AC-22 · pass · Reversal of the disabled-model flag fails the profiles web test that checks the Settings link.
- AC-23 · pass · Partial: the exhausted-model skip reversal fails the scheduler tests; the resume reset was covered only by the runs test, not reversed.
- AC-24 · pass · Injecting an interruption of live Runs on profile save fails the dispatcher test that asserts the Run stays live.
- AC-25 · pass · Reversal of the verify Sonnet default fails the domain test.
- AC-26 · fail · A timeout that calls failModel stays green: the test at watcher.test.ts:598 does not wire a real failModel, so no fallback can occur in it.
- AC-27 · pass · Renaming a key in en.ts fails the dictionary parity tests.

#### Reviewers

- `lunasq-security-reviewer` · NO FINDINGS
- `lunasq-ux-reviewer` · 4 findings
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/projects.tsx:53 · AC-27 · The missing-profile badge shows raw phase keys in the Spanish UI.
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/task-detail.tsx:92 · AC-13 · The Edit-the-project link goes to /projects, not to that project's form.
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/project-form-dialog.tsx:279 · AC-7 · The profile select has no loading or error state; it shows no profile while loading or when the request fails.
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/profile-form-dialog.tsx:192 · AC-22 · A disabled-provider model in the profile form has no link to Settings.
- `lunasq-ui-reviewer` · 3 findings
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/projects.tsx:52 · AC-12 · The badge uses whitespace-nowrap, so the long missing-phase list can overflow a 320px card.
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/profile-form-dialog.tsx:194 · AC-27 · The model and effort row does not stack at small widths, so the model input shrinks to about 40px.
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/profiles.tsx:54 · AC-27 · A long model id does not wrap in the card.
- `lunasq-code-reviewer` · 3 findings
  - [minor] packages/domain/src/scheduler.ts:314 · AC-19 · An exhausted profile is held as queued when the provider is disabled, but escalates when the same case happens during launch.
  - [minor] packages/db/src/runs.ts:777 · AC-19 · A pause right after a fallback clears the exhausted set, so the primary model is retried.
  - [minor] packages/server/src/dispatcher/dispatcher.ts:359 · AC-17 · failModelRun ignores the result of failModel and reports a fallback that did not happen.

#### Must-fix

- [major] packages/server/src/reconciler/watcher.test.ts:598 · AC-26 · The phase-timeout test does not inject the real failModel, so a timeout that calls the model-fallback path stays green. Wire failModel (for example as a spy that returns fallback) and assert that expire does not call it and that no model is failed. Then revert expire's guard and confirm the test fails on that assertion. · source: approver

#### Refuted findings

- [major] packages/server/src/dispatcher/dispatcher.test.ts:712 · AC-19 · No start-path test has a model that failed after launch in the same dispatch; removing exhaustedModels from the start-loop tried set keeps all tests green. · source: lunasq-code-reviewer
  - Reason: Reversal: removing the exhaustedModels line from the tried set fails 2 dispatcher tests (a launched Run that fails because of its model moves the phase on; the last enabled model escalates with every failure). The invariant is covered.

```
export function resolvePhaseModel(
  phase: Phase,
  profile: AgentProfile | null | undefined,
  enabled: AgentProviderSettings,
  exhausted: Iterable<number> = [],
): PhaseModelResolution {
  if (!profile) return { status: "unassigned" };
  const spent = new Set(exhausted);
  let anyEnabled = false;
  for (const [index, model] of profile.models.entries()) {
    if (!isModelEnabled(model, enabled)) continue;
    anyEnabled = true;
    if (spent.has(index)) continue;
    return {
      status: "ready",
      profileId: profile.id,
      index,
      model: withPhaseDefaults(phase, model),
    };
  }
  return { status: anyEnabled ? "exhausted" : "providers_disabled" };
}

----
}

… 725 characters omitted …
n create and update (400 `invalid_input`, path `["models", i, "args"]`).
- **Projects select a profile per phase.** `Project.agentProfiles` is `{ refine, implement, verify, deliver }`, each a profile id or `null`. The project no longer carries provider, model, effort or args. Per‑phase timeouts and limits stay in the project.
- **Unassigned phases hold their tasks.** A phase with `null` has no model: the scheduler does not dispatch its tasks (they stay `queued`, with no Run, retry or escalation) and `claimRun` rejects with `profile_missing`. Creating a project leaves every phase unassigned. Migration `0018_agent_profiles` sets every existing project's phases to `null`, so the human must pick profiles after upgrading.

 Runs. A phase dispatch ends
+ * with a Run that did not fail or with a resume, so the next one starts again from the top of the list.
+ */
+export function exhaustedModelIndexes(db: Reader, taskId: string, phase: Phase): number[] {
+  const chain = dispatchRuns(db, taskId, phase);
+  if (chain.length === 0) return [];
+  const ids = new Set(chain.map((r) => r.id));
+  const exhausted = new Set<number>();
+  for (const run of chain)
+    if (run.modelFailed && run.modelIndex !== null) exhausted.add(run.modelIndex);
+  for (const a of providerAttemptsOf(db, taskId, (id) => ids.has(id))) {
+    if (a.status === "failed") exhausted.add(a.modelIndex);
+  }
+  return [...exhausted].sort((x, y) => x - y);
 }

# Agent profiles with model fallbacks

**Status:** accepted. It replaces the per‑project, per‑phase `{ kind, model, effort, args }` profile of [ADR 0008](0008-config-proyecto.md) and [effort per phase](effort-por-fase.md), and the registry‑order fallback of [ADR 0016](0016-proveedores-agente.md) and [settings‑proveedores](settings-proveedores.md).

## Context

A project configured one provider, model, effort and args per phase, and the only fallback was "try the other registered providers in registry order" when a CLI did not start. When a model hit its rate limit or usage limit, or the agent exited, the task retried the same model or escalated. The human had no way to say "try this other model next", and the same configuration had to be co

export const agentProviders = new AgentProviderRegistry([
  claudeProvider,
  copilotProvider,
  codexProvider,
]);

/**
 * Checks a profile model against its provider's reserved flags (the ones LunasQ sets itself) without
 * building a command line. Throws `AgentProviderError` naming the conflicting flag.
 */
export function validateProfileModel(model: ProfileModel): void {
  agentProviders.get(model.kind).args(model, {
    mcpConfigPath: "/validate/mcp.json",
    mcpUrl: "http://127.0.0.1/validate",
    settingsPath: "/validate/settings.json",
    sessionId: "validate",
  });
}

#### Criteria

- AC-1 · pass · Profile create/list tests in db, server and web pass; name-conflict reversal fails db tests (previous round)
- AC-2 · pass · Reversal of the conflict code fails db repository tests for repeated name and 0 or >10 models
- AC-3 · pass · Reversal of the effort/--effort guard fails adapters tests naming the flag
- AC-4 · pass · Reversal of the in-use guard fails db test and server 409 test
- AC-5 · pass · Reversal of the delete statement fails the db test that records agent_profile.deleted
- AC-6 · pass · Reversal of the profile_not_found mapping fails POST and PATCH server tests
- AC-7 · pass · Reversal removing the no-profile option fails 3 web project tests; UX minor on silent query failure is non-blocking
- AC-8 · pass · Reversal of the 0018 migration UPDATE fails schema tests
- AC-9 · pass · Reversal of the unassigned resolve fails 3 domain tests (previous round, same production code)
- AC-10 · pass · Reversal of the profile_missing check in claimRun: test rejects with profile_missing naming the phase fails on assertion
- AC-11 · pass · Reversal of the banner empty check fails 3 banner tests
- AC-12 · fail · Missing-profile Badge has whitespace-nowrap (badge.tsx:6) and lists all unassigned phases at projects.tsx:52-53; overflows a 320px card. Must-fix from lunasq-ui-reviewer.
- AC-13 · pass · Reversal hiding the hold notice fails 2 task-detail tests; link target is minor
- AC-14 · pass · Reversal of the project.updated event type fails the db projects test
- AC-15 · pass · Skip of exhausted and disabled models fails scheduler and agent-profile tests; primary-start Run test not reversed separately
- AC-16 · pass · Forcing model index 1 out of the start chain fails 6 dispatcher tests
- AC-17 · pass · Reversal reporting model failure as unavailable: 'usage limit with another model...' watcher test fails on assertion (this round)
- AC-18 · pass · Same reversal: 'the agent exits...' and 'stops without the end-of-phase signal' watcher tests fail on assertion (this round)
- AC-19 · pass · Reversal of the last-model escalation fails 'last model ... escalates with providers_unavailable' watcher test; exhausted-skip reversal fails domain/scheduler tests. Held-instead-of-escalated gap when next model's provider is disabled is a minor finding
- AC-20 · pass · Reversal of the disabled-model skip fails 'with the primary disabled it creates the Run with the next model' and domain scheduler tests (this round)
- AC-21 · pass · Reversal of the disabled-model skip fails 'with every model of the profile disabled it rejects' and 'holds the task when every model belongs to a disabled provider' (this round)
- AC-22 · pass · Reversal of the disabled-model flag fails the profiles web test checking the Settings link
- AC-23 · pass · Exhausted-model skip reversal fails scheduler tests; resume reset covered by runs test (not reversed separately)
- AC-24 · pass · Injecting interruption of live Runs fails the dispatcher test asserting the Run stays live
- AC-25 · pass · Reversal of the verify Sonnet default fails the domain test
- AC-26 · pass · Reversal (expire calls failModel) fails 'a phase timeout is not a model failure' on expect(failModel).not.toHaveBeenCalled(): assertion error, 1 call
- AC-27 · pass · Renaming a key in en.ts fails dictionary parity tests

443	  /** Phase timeout expired: needs_human with notification; the agent stays alive. */
444	  private async expire(w: Watch): Promise<void> {
445	    if (this.inPhase(w, "waiting_human")) {
446	      // It expired with the question open before the outbox paused the clock: it stays paused
447	      // without budget and expires as soon as the answer resumes it.
448	      w.clock = new PhaseClock(0, () => this.serial(w.runId, (w) => this.expire(w)));
449	      w.clock.pause("waiting_human");
450	      w.clock.start();
451	      return;
452	    }
453	    w.expired = true;
454	    clearTimeout(w.grace);

// SSE change stream for the UI (ADR uw-21-front-end). Each task change leaves an
// event in the Event log, so the stream follows the id of the last event: with new events it sends
// `change` with the affected tasks and the front end invalidates those queries. The message id is

import { cn } from "../../lib/utils.ts";

export function Card({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"section">) {
  return <section className={cn("card", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function CardHeader({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"header">) {
  return <header className={cn("flex flex-col gap-1 p-4 pb-2", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function CardTitle({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"h2">) {
  return (
    <h2
      className={cn("text-base font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function CardContent({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return <div className={cn("p-4 pt-2", className)} {...props} />;
}

### Verdict

- Verdict: **pass**
- Risk: medium

#### Criteria

- AC-1 · pass · Create/list tests in db, server and web pass; reversal of the name-conflict code fails db tests.
- AC-2 · pass · Reversal of the conflict code fails db tests for repeated name and 0 or >10 models.
- AC-3 · pass · Reversal of the effort/--effort guard fails adapters tests naming the flag.
- AC-4 · pass · Reversal of the in-use guard fails the db test and the server 409 test.
- AC-5 · pass · Reversal of the delete statement fails the db test that records agent_profile.deleted.
- AC-6 · pass · Reversal of the profile_not_found mapping fails the POST and PATCH server tests.
- AC-7 · pass · Reversal removing the no-profile option fails 3 web project tests.
- AC-8 · pass · Reversal of the 0018 migration UPDATE fails schema tests.
- AC-9 · pass · Reversal of the unassigned-phase resolve fails 3 domain tests (same production code as the previous round).
- AC-10 · pass · Reversal of the profile_missing check in claimRun fails the db runs test on assertion.
- AC-11 · pass · Reversal of the banner empty check fails 3 banner tests.
- AC-12 · pass · This round: restoring the pre-fix badge class fails 'the missing-profile badge wraps inside the card' on assertion (expected className to contain whitespace-normal). UI reviewer reports no findings on the fix.
- AC-13 · pass · Reversal hiding the hold notice fails 2 task-detail tests.
- AC-14 · pass · Reversal of the project.updated event type fails the db projects test.
- AC-15 · pass · Reversal of the skip of exhausted and disabled models fails scheduler and agent-profile tests. The primary-start Run test was not reversed separately (see knownRisks).
- AC-16 · pass · Forcing model index 1 out of the start chain fails 6 dispatcher tests.
- AC-17 · pass · Reporting model failure as unavailable fails the watcher and dispatcher usage-limit tests on assertion.
- AC-18 · pass · Same reversal fails the watcher tests for exit and stop without the end-of-phase signal.
- AC-19 · pass · Reversal of the last-model escalation fails the providers_unavailable watcher test; reversal of the exhausted skip fails domain and scheduler tests.
- AC-20 · pass · Reversal of the disabled-model skip fails the 'primary disabled, next model' Run test and domain and scheduler tests.
- AC-21 · pass · Same reversal fails 'every model disabled rejects with the task queued' and the all-disabled hold test.
- AC-22 · pass · Reversal of the disabled-model flag fails the profiles web test that checks the Settings link.
- AC-23 · pass · This round: removing the resume boundary in dispatchRuns fails 'accumulates exhausted models and resets them with a resume' on assertion (expected [0,1] to deeply equal []).
- AC-24 · pass · Injecting an interruption of live Runs on profile save fails the dispatcher test that asserts the Run stays live.
- AC-25 · pass · Reversal of the verify Sonnet default fails the domain test.
- AC-26 · pass · This round: calling failModel in expire() fails 'a phase timeout is not a model failure' on expect(failModel).not.toHaveBeenCalled(): called 1 times.
- AC-27 · pass · Renaming a key in en.ts fails the dictionary parity tests.

#### Reviewers

- `lunasq-security-reviewer` · skipped · The delta since the last verdict (f2b6200, 1e63d3c) changes only watcher.test.ts and the web projects files; no trust boundary is touched, and the full-diff security review of the previous round had no findings.
- `lunasq-ux-reviewer` · skipped · The delta changes only the badge's CSS class in projects.tsx and one test; no flow, state or visible text changes since the last verdict.
- `lunasq-ui-reviewer` · NO FINDINGS
- `lunasq-code-reviewer` · skipped · The only test change since the last verdict is the phase-timeout test, whose reversal was run by the approver; the rest of the code is unchanged since the last full review.
